Report Matrix!A1	ELECTRONIC PAYROLL SUPPORT DOCUMENT — REPORT MATRIX
Report Matrix!A3	Report ID
Report Matrix!B3	CO-DIAN-001
Report Matrix!A4	Report Name (EN)
Report Matrix!B4	Electronic Payroll Support Document
Report Matrix!A5	Report Name (Local)
Report Matrix!B5	Documento Soporte de Pago de Nomina Electronica
Report Matrix!A6	Document ID
Report Matrix!B6	CO-DIAN-001
Report Matrix!A7	Document Title
Report Matrix!B7	Electronic Payroll Support Document - Field Specification
Report Matrix!A8	Country
Report Matrix!B8	Colombia
Report Matrix!A9	Authority
Report Matrix!B9	National Tax and Customs Directorate (DIAN)
Report Matrix!A10	Report Type
Report Matrix!B10	Statutory electronic tax document, one per employee per month
Report Matrix!A11	Tax Type
Report Matrix!B11	Payroll source document underlying income-tax withholding and social-security reporting
Report Matrix!A12	Relationship
Report Matrix!B12	Employer to DIAN, machine-to-machine
Report Matrix!A13	Frequency
Report Matrix!B13	Monthly - ONE document per employee per month, irrespective of the pay cycle (Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.2.2)
Report Matrix!A14	Submission Deadline
Report Matrix!B14	FIRST TEN DAYS of the month following the payment or credit in account (Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.4.1.1)
Report Matrix!A15	File Format
Report Matrix!B15	XML (UBL 2.1)
Report Matrix!A16	File Extension
Report Matrix!B16	.xml
Report Matrix!A17	Character Encoding
Report Matrix!B17	UTF-8 - mandatory; any other encoding is rejected (Annex v1.0 rule ZB03)
Report Matrix!A18	Record Types / Sections
Report Matrix!B18	SOAP 1.2 envelope; NominaIndividual and NominaIndividualDeAjuste document types
Report Matrix!A19	Version
Report Matrix!B19	v1.0
Report Matrix!A22	DETAILED COMPLIANCE NOTES (Colombia-specific)
Report Matrix!A23	SUBMISSION CHANNEL
Report Matrix!A24	- Channel: xml (machine-to-machine) | Portal: DIAN Sistema de Validacion y Gestion de Documents, catalogo-vpfe.dian.gov.co | Source: Technical Annex v1.0 s.9.3 to s.9.8 (SOAP 1.2, Document/Literal, TLS 1.2 mutual authentication, WS-Security 1.0 X.509 Token Profile 1.1).
Report Matrix!A25	- This is the ONE Colombian payroll filing that is genuinely machine-to-machine. It inverts the Colombian default: Form 350, Form 220 and Format 2276 are portal round-trips, and PILA is filed through a licensed private information operator. Do not generalise the M2M posture from this filing to the others.
Report Matrix!A26	- Operation SendNominaSync takes a single parameter, wcf:contentFile, carrying the base64 of a ZIP that contains the signed XML. Maximum 500 KB. Annex s.9.5 and s.9.7.2 print the exact envelope, reproduced on Sheet 9.
Report Matrix!A27	- Enablement (habilitacion) with DIAN is a PROJECT PREREQUISITE with a lead time, not a configuration step: global validation rule 92 rejects any document from a non-enabled issuer, and rule 90 permits a given document number only once per employee.
Report Matrix!A28	INTEGRATION TYPE AND DIRECTION
Report Matrix!A29	- Integration: outbound file generation and inbound response handling. Outbound: the signed XML. Inbound: the synchronous base64 ApplicationResponse in the same call.
Report Matrix!A30	- The inbound leg (CO-IN-NE-APPRESPONSE) is specified in this workbook rather than in a separate file, because it is not an independent government-to-employer flow - it is the response half of a single synchronous exchange and shares this filing's identifiers. See the section 'INBOUND - DIAN VALIDATION RESPONSE' on the Spec sheet.
Report Matrix!A31	- product_mapping_required = true. The XML separates salary from non-salary at ELEMENT level with paired S / NS element names, so every wage type carries a salary / non-salary attribute in the Wage Type Catalogue (WTC). The engine reads that attribute; the split is never derived at reporting time from the amount, the description or the wage-type name.
Report Matrix!A32	RESIDENT / NON-RESIDENT TREATMENT
Report Matrix!A33	- RESIDENT: RetencionFuente (NIE177) carries the ET art. 383 progressive table (0 / 19 / 28 / 33 / 35 / 37 / 39% on the tax-unit bands 0-95, 95-150, 150-360, 360-640, 640-945, 945-2,300, above 2,300 UVT), applied to the art. 388 depurated monthly base under Procedure 1 or Procedure 2.
Report Matrix!A34	- NON-RESIDENT: RetencionFuente (NIE177) carries the FLAT 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS payment - no art. 388 depuration, no 25% exempt income, no art. 387 deductions, no tax-free band. Confirmed by DIAN Concept 011108 of 29 June 2026, upholding Concept 1789 (int. 14595) of 2025.
Report Matrix!A35	- The XML has NO residence attribute. Residence is therefore invisible to DIAN in this document and visible only through the amount in NIE177 and, on Form 350, through the sheet-2 payments-al-exterior section. The engine must carry a residence flag with an effective date on the employee record, re-evaluate it EVERY period, and switch the schedule PROSPECTIVELY from the payment following the date the 183-day test is met. Never retroactively - there is no payroll true-up of the earlier months.
Report Matrix!A36	- Where the non-resident's work is performed ENTIRELY outside Colombia the income is foreign-source and not subject to withholding at all (DIAN Official letter 28380 of 2019); LugarTrabajoPais (NIE050) is the field that signals this.
Report Matrix!A37	- Treaty relief can reduce or eliminate the 20% where a double-tax treaty assigns taxing rights to the residence state. Mercans applies treaty relief ONLY on written client instruction supported by a residence certificate.
Report Matrix!A38	RELATED FILINGS / CROSS-REFERENCES
Report Matrix!A39	- The monthly sum of RetencionFuente (NIE177) across all employees must reconcile to Form 350 casilla 93 (withholdings - employment income, natural persons) plus casilla 94 (withholdings - pension income) for the same period - CO-DIAN-002.
Report Matrix!A40	- Salud (NIE163), FondoPension (NIE166) and FondoSP (NIE168 / NIE170) must reconcile to the corresponding PILA Record Tipo 2 fields 55, 47 and 51 / 52 - CO-PILA-003 - subject to the different rounding conventions.
Report Matrix!A41	- Severance savings (NIE120) and PagoIntereses (NIE122) feed Form 220 casillas 47-49 and Format 2276 numerals 18-20 - CO-DIAN-004 and CO-DIAN-005.
Report Matrix!A42	- The annual accumulation of every Earnings element feeds Form 220 casillas 36-52 and Format 2276 numerals 5-22.
Report Matrix!A43	- The graphical representation (payslip) is PERMISSIVE under Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.8.3, but if issued it must carry the thirteen items of art. 1.5.3.3.1 and a QR of at least 2 cm on EVERY page. Colombia has NO statutory payslip-content article in its labour code - the itemisation rule is this tax resolution. See 06-payslip.
Payroll Scope Summary!A1	PAYROLL SCOPE SUMMARY - ELECTRONIC PAYROLL SUPPORT DOCUMENT
Payroll Scope Summary!A2	PURPOSE: Tell the developer WHO gets a Electronic Payroll Support Document each month and what happens to a borderline case.
Payroll Scope Summary!A4	SCENARIO
Payroll Scope Summary!B4	REPORT?
Payroll Scope Summary!C4	NOTES / REASONING
Payroll Scope Summary!A5	═══ WHO GETS REPORTED ═══
Payroll Scope Summary!A6	Indefinite-term employee (indefinite-term contract)
Payroll Scope Summary!B6	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C6	The core private-sector population. CST art. 47; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 5.
Payroll Scope Summary!A7	Fixed-term employee (fixed-term contract, max 4 years)
Payroll Scope Summary!B7	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C7	Identical reporting treatment to an indefinite-term contract. CST art. 46; Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 6.
Payroll Scope Summary!A8	Specific-work or task employee (contract por obra o labor)
Payroll Scope Summary!B8	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C8	An employment contract; CST art. 46. Electronic payroll TipoContrato = 3 (Obra o Labor).
Payroll Scope Summary!A9	Occasional / accidental / transitory worker (< 1 month, non-core activity)
Payroll Scope Summary!B9	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C9	Still an employee under CST art. 6. Reported on every filing; the PILA return type and the day counts differ, not the obligation.
Payroll Scope Summary!A10	Part-time employee
Payroll Scope Summary!B10	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C10	Colombia has no separate part-time regime. Reported normally; the PILA contribution base is floored at 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905) except for the worker type 51 partial-time regime.
Payroll Scope Summary!A11	All-inclusive salary employee (all-inclusive salary, >= 13 SMMLV)
Payroll Scope Summary!B11	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C11	CST art. 132 num. 2. Reported with the integral-salary flag set; the contribution base is 70% of the integral salary, and the electronic payroll carries SalarioIntegral = true.
Payroll Scope Summary!A12	Apprentice (apprenticeship contract)
Payroll Scope Summary!B12	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C12	Law 2466 of 2025 art. 21 converted the apprenticeship into a special fixed-term LABOUR contract, regulated by Decree 0223 of 5 March 2026. Electronic payroll TipoContrato = 4; PILA tipo cotizante 19 'Aprendices etapa lectiva Ley 2466 de 2025' for the lectiva phase from the health period of August 2025 (code 12 only for earlier periods). For health periods before August 2025, code 19 covers the productive phase; from the health period of August 2025, AT2 v30 names no code for the productive phase (AT2 v30 §2.1.2.3.1 campo 5, types 12 and 19).
Payroll Scope Summary!A13	Intern / practical placement (practicas o pasantias)
Payroll Scope Summary!B13	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C13	Electronic payroll TipoContrato = 5. Reported where remunerated.
Payroll Scope Summary!A14	Teleworkers and remote workers
Payroll Scope Summary!B14	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C14	Law 1221 of 2008 as amended by Law 2466 of 2025 arts. 52-54. No reporting difference; the electronic payroll carries a dedicated Teletrabajo earning element (NIE158).
Payroll Scope Summary!A15	Resident foreign national under a Colombian contract
Payroll Scope Summary!B15	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C15	Full reporting. Withheld under the ET art. 383 progressive table once tax residence is acquired.
Payroll Scope Summary!A16	NON-RESIDENT foreign national under a Colombian contract
Payroll Scope Summary!B16	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C16	IN SCOPE and frequently mis-configured. Withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS, with no art. 388 depuration, until the 183-day residence test is met (DIAN Concept 011108 of 29 June 2026). See the resident / non-resident block in the Report Matrix.
Payroll Scope Summary!A17	Employed old-age pensioner in active service
Payroll Scope Summary!B17	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C17	PILA worker sub-type 01; no pension contribution (PILA pension rate field = 0), health and the levies still apply.
Payroll Scope Summary!A18	Executive director / statutory legal representative holding an employment contract
Payroll Scope Summary!B18	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C18	Reported as an ordinary employee where an employment contract exists.
Payroll Scope Summary!A19	Independent contractor (service contract)
Payroll Scope Summary!B19	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C19	Not an employee. The contractor self-files PILA on 40% of fees (return types I / Y, descoped) and is reported by the payer in the third-party information return, not in this employer filing. Withholding on their fees does pass through Form 350, but under a different casilla.
Payroll Scope Summary!A20	Public-sector employee (contributor type 47, SGP-funded entity)
Payroll Scope Summary!B20	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C20	Public-sector only. The PILA return types T and F of public-sector entities are outside the scope of this payroll, so worker type 47 is never emitted.
Payroll Scope Summary!A21	Non-executive director paid fees only (junta directiva)
Payroll Scope Summary!B21	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C21	Fees of a non-executive director (honorarios de junta directiva) are not derived from an employment relationship, so they are outside the document, which supports only payments derived from an employment or statutory relationship and pensions borne by the employer (Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.2.1). They are paid and withheld outside payroll and reported by the payer in the third-party information return, as for an independent contractor (see the contractor row). A director who also holds an employment contract is reported for that contract only (see the executive-director row).
Payroll Scope Summary!A22	Pensioner: pension paid by a fund, or paid directly by the employer
Payroll Scope Summary!B22	VARIES
Payroll Scope Summary!C22	Paid by a fund: NO - the paying fund is the withholding agent and the reporting party, not the employer. Paid directly by the employer (pensioner 'a cargo del empleador'): YES - the document supports the payments 'que se desprenden de una relación laboral o legal y reglamentaria y los pensionados a cargo del empleador', the employer that makes payments 'a los pensionados a cargo del empleador' is obliged to issue it, and the pensioner is a beneficiary of the payment (Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 arts. 1.5.3.2.1, 1.5.3.2.3 and 1.5.3.1.1 num. 5).
Payroll Scope Summary!A23	═══ AMOUNTS AND CONCEPTS REPORTED ═══
Payroll Scope Summary!A24	Basic salary for the days worked
Payroll Scope Summary!B24	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C24	SueldoTrabajado (NIE070). The single largest input to every downstream filing.
Payroll Scope Summary!A25	Statutory transport allowance
Payroll Scope Summary!B25	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C25	AuxilioTransporte (NIE071), where the employee earns up to 2 SMMLV. Not salary for contributions, but IS in the base of cesantias, prima and the severance indemnity.
Payroll Scope Summary!A26	Overtime and premium hours, itemised by type with times and percentages
Payroll Scope Summary!B26	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C26	Seven separate hour-type blocks (table 5.5.5), each with start time, end time, quantity, percentage and amount. A single aggregated 'overtime' figure is NOT admissible.
Payroll Scope Summary!A27	Vacation taken and vacation compensated in cash, as separate elements
Payroll Scope Summary!B27	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C27	VacacionesComunes and VacacionesCompensadas. They must not be merged. Vacation paid in cash (during employment or on termination) is NOT part of the IBC for health, pension and occupational risk (Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, exp. 26941 of 2024-04-04; unification ruling exp. 25185 of 2021, rule 1), while for enjoyed vacation days health and pension are contributed on the IBC of the month before the vacation starts (DUR 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.5.1; Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, exp. 25958 of 2022). Both enjoyed vacation and vacation paid in cash ARE part of the parafiscal base for CCF, SENA and ICBF (Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17; Ley 89 de 1988 art. 1 par. 1; Consejo de Estado, Sala de Consulta, concept 2027 of 2010).
Payroll Scope Summary!A28	Service bonus, severance savings and interest on severance savings
Payroll Scope Summary!B28	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C28	Primas, Severance savings and Cesantias/@PagoIntereses. Reported here even though severance savings are excluded from the monthly withholding base.
Payroll Scope Summary!A29	Incapacity, maternity / paternity leave and paid leave
Payroll Scope Summary!B29	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C29	Each with its own dates, quantity and amount, and - for incapacity - its type code.
Payroll Scope Summary!A30	Unpaid leave and lawful strike
Payroll Scope Summary!B30	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C30	Reported as days ONLY. Both blocks deliberately carry no amount element.
Payroll Scope Summary!A31	Benefits in kind (salary in kind)
Payroll Scope Summary!B31	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C31	Through the paired OtroConcepto S / NS elements or the specific allowance elements. Valued contractually or by expert appraisal; Colombia has no notional valuation scale.
Payroll Scope Summary!A32	Employer social-security contributions
Payroll Scope Summary!B32	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C32	The document reports what the EMPLOYEE accrued and what was DEDUCTED from the employee. The employer's 12% pension, 8.5% health, occupational-risk premium and 9% levies are an employer cost reported on PILA, never a deduction here.
Payroll Scope Summary!A33	Employer payroll levies (SENA 2%, ICBF 3%, CCF 4%)
Payroll Scope Summary!B33	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C33	Employer cost, reported on PILA. Never an employee deduction.
Payroll Scope Summary!A34	Documented business-expense reimbursements
Payroll Scope Summary!B34	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C34	A reimbursement of an expense the employee incurred on the employer's behalf, supported by the supplier's document, is not remuneration (CST art. 128: amounts received not for the employee's benefit but to carry out the job, such as transport, work tools and similar, are not salary) and is not an accrual of the employee; it is settled through accounts payable, not through this document. Per diems for meals and lodging ARE reported, in ViaticoManuAlojS (permanent, salary) or ViaticoManuAlojNS (accidental, non-salary) - NIE072 / NIE073.
Payroll Scope Summary!A35	Amounts in a currency other than the Colombian peso
Payroll Scope Summary!B35	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C35	Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.8.5 makes the peso mandatory. A foreign currency may be ADDED to a graphical representation but never substituted; the TRM element (NIE200) exists in the schema but is out of scope for a Colombian payroll.
Payroll Scope Summary!A36	═══ TIMING AND RELATIONSHIP ═══
Payroll Scope Summary!A37	Reported on the accrual (causacion) date
Payroll Scope Summary!B37	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C37	Every Colombian payroll filing is keyed to the PAYMENT or credit-in-account date, not to the accrual date. A December salary paid in January falls in the January filing.
Payroll Scope Summary!A38	Reported on the payment or credit-in-account date
Payroll Scope Summary!B38	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C38	The universal Colombian rule - payment or credit in account. Applies to the electronic payroll (DIAN Resolution 000227 of 2025 art. 1.5.3.2.2), to Form 350, to Form 220 and to Format 2276.
Payroll Scope Summary!A39	A semi-monthly payroll produces two filings a month
Payroll Scope Summary!B39	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C39	It does not. The electronic payroll is expressly ONE monthly document per employee 'irrespective of whether the obliged party makes payments in different or shorter periods' (Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.2.2). PILA and Form 350 are likewise monthly.
Payroll Scope Summary!A40	Nil period still filed
Payroll Scope Summary!B40	NO
Payroll Scope Summary!C40	The document is the support of the payments or credits in account made to the employee in the month (Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 arts. 1.5.3.2.1 and 1.5.3.2.2); it is not a periodic return. Where no payment or credit in account is made to an employee in the month (for example a full-month unpaid leave or suspension), no document is generated for that employee, and there is no nil document at employer level. Where payments exist but the deductions absorb them, a document IS generated with ComprobanteTotal 0.00 (see the zero-net-pay row).
Payroll Scope Summary!A41	Corrections handled by re-filing the whole period
Payroll Scope Summary!B41	VARIES
Payroll Scope Summary!C41	Electronic payroll: NO - a dedicated adjustment note (NominaIndividualDeAjuste, Replace or Delete) referencing the original CUNE. Form 350: yes, a corrective return. PILA: a correction return type N (correcciones) for the period, filed through the same operador as the original return; the C records (correct values) must be equal to or greater than the A records (original values) in IBC and in days, and type N cannot be used for a correction that leads to a refund, which is requested from each administradora outside PILA (AT2 v30 §2.1.1.2.2 campo 8; Disposiciones comunes a la Planilla N). A return paid after its deadline is filed on type E; type M (mora) applies only to periods before health period March 2014 with a total absence of payment (AT2 v30 §2.1.1.2.2 campo 8).
Payroll Scope Summary!A42	═══ SPECIAL CASES ═══
Payroll Scope Summary!A43	Employee with zero net pay (deductions equal accrued)
Payroll Scope Summary!B43	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C43	A document is still required. ComprobanteTotal (NIE189) is 0.00; the accrued and deducted blocks are fully itemised.
Payroll Scope Summary!A44	Employee whose withholding falls in the 0% band
Payroll Scope Summary!B44	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C44	The payment is still reported. RetencionFuente (NIE177) is simply omitted rather than zero-filled.
Payroll Scope Summary!A45	Mid-month hire or mid-month leaver
Payroll Scope Summary!B45	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C45	DiasTrabajados (NIE069) is reduced; FechaIngreso or FechaRetiro is set; TiempoLaborado is computed to the settlement end on the 360/30 convention.
Payroll Scope Summary!A46	Second employer of a multi-employer employee
Payroll Scope Summary!B46	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C46	Each employer issues its own document for its own payments. There is no aggregation across employers at document level.
Payroll Scope Summary!A47	Employee paid semi-monthly
Payroll Scope Summary!B47	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C47	ONE monthly document carrying PeriodoNomina = 4 (Fortnightly) and TWO FechaPago (NIE203) occurrences. Not two documents.
Payroll Scope Summary!A48	Correction of a document already validated by DIAN
Payroll Scope Summary!B48	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C48	By adjustment note (NominaIndividualDeAjuste), never by re-sending the original number - global rule 90 forbids reuse.
Payroll Scope Summary!A49	Employee holding a PPT (Temporary protection permit)
Payroll Scope Summary!B49	YES
Payroll Scope Summary!C49	Reported. Annex table 5.2.1 has no code for the PPT (Decree 216 of 2021 post-dates the annex); a PPT holder is reported with code 47, the residence-permit code of the table, and the PPT number in NumeroDocumento; a PEP holder is reported with code 47, the PEP code of the table (see NIE044).

If a graphical representation (payslip PDF) is issued, this QR must be printed at not less than 2 cm x 2 cm on EVERY page (DIAN Resolution 000227 of 2025 art. 1.5.3.8.3 and Annex s.13.1).

Nomina Electronica Spec!A12	CUNE - unique payroll document code
Nomina Electronica Spec!B12	CUNE - Codigo Unico de Nomina Electronica
Nomina Electronica Spec!C12	General Information
Nomina Electronica Spec!D12	NIE024
Nomina Electronica Spec!E12	N
Nomina Electronica Spec!F12	Y
Nomina Electronica Spec!H12	/NominaIndividual/InformacionGeneral/@CUNE
Nomina Electronica Spec!I12	96
Nomina Electronica Spec!J12	A (hex, 96 chars)
Nomina Electronica Spec!K12	16560dc8956122e84ffb743c817fe7d494e058a44d9ca3fa4c234c268b4f766003253fbee7ea4af9682dd57210f3bac2
Nomina Electronica Spec!L12	System.CUNE
Nomina Electronica Spec!M12	COMPOSITE KEY - position breakdown. The CUNE is the SHA-384 hexadecimal digest (96 hex characters) of ELEVEN concatenated inputs, in this exact order and with no separators (Technical Annex s.8.1.1.1): [1] NumNE = Prefix + Sequence number (NIE012); [2] FecNE = FechaGen (NIE026, AAAA-MM-DD); [3] HorNE = HoraGen including the GMT offset (NIE027); [4] ValDev = DevengadosTotal (NIE187), decimal point, 2 decimals TRUNCATED, no thousand separators, no currency symbol; [5] ValDed = DeduccionesTotal (NIE188), same format; [6] ValTolNE = ComprobanteTotal (NIE189), same format; [7] NitNE = employer NIT (NIE033) without dots, hyphens or the verification digit; [8] DocEmp = employee document number (NIE045) without dots or hyphens; [9] TipoXML (NIE202); [10] Software-Pin - the secret PIN, which NEVER appears in the XML; [11] TipAmb = Ambiente (NIE023). PERSISTENCE: the CUNE is immutable once the document is validated. Any change to any of the eleven inputs produces a different CUNE and therefore a different document; corrections are made by an adjustment note carrying its own CUNE and referencing the original.
Nomina Electronica Spec!N12	N
Nomina Electronica Spec!O12	-
Nomina Electronica Spec!P12	Not an amount - no rounding.
Nomina Electronica Spec!Q12	N
Nomina Electronica Spec!R12	Identifier

Nomina Electronica Spec!M56	Annex table 5.2.1: 11 Record civil, 12 Minor ID card, 13 Citizenship ID card, 21 Tarjeta de extranjeria, 22 Cedula de extranjeria, 31 NIT, 41 Passport, 42 Foreign identity document, 47 PEP, 50 NIT de otro pais, 91 NUIP (employee only). Codes 21 and 22 are both valid: 22 is reported where the employee holds a cedula de extranjeria and 21 where the employee holds a tarjeta de extranjeria; the code follows the document the employee holds, as recorded in the document-type field. A holder of a PEP (Permiso Especial de Permanencia) is reported with code 47, the PEP code of annex table 5.2.1. The table has no code for the PPT (Permiso por Protección Temporal, Decree 216 of 2021, created after the annex): a PPT holder is reported with code 47, the residence-permit code of the table, and the PPT number in NumeroDocumento (NIE045). |  | Source: the HR master stores no table 5.2.1 code; it holds one field per document type ('Citizenship Card ID', 'Indentity Card for Foreigners', 'Identity Card', 'National ID'). The codes for 'Identity Card' and 'National ID', and the precedence applied where more than one document field is filled, are maintained in the same document-type mapping table, so that exactly one document type is reported and the same document drives NIE044 and NIE045.

Strip dots and hyphens. A document number that carries leading zeros must be emitted exactly as held in the HR master and used in the same form as CUNE input [8]; xsd:integer typing drops leading zeros, so such a number is subject to the same schema defect.

Nomina Electronica Spec!L68	Payroll.Contractual_Salary [WTC: CO-SALARIO-BASICO]
Nomina Electronica Spec!M68	The amount is owned by the Wage Type Catalogue, not by the Data Dictionary (scope rule: 'Base Salary / Basic Salary amounts -> WTC, NOT DD'). The 'Payroll.' prefix marks it as outside DD master data; it is a standing contract attribute, not a per-run computation. Column E is therefore N and the Data Dictionary holds no Contractual Salary field. The CONTRACTUAL monthly salary, not the amount actually paid for the period. It stays constant across a month with unpaid leave or a mid-month hire, whereas SueldoTrabajado (NIE070) moves. Never below 1 SMMLV (COP 1,750,905 in 2026) for a full-time employee. |  | Source: pay-run field 'Salary Assigned' (the contractual monthly salary, including an integral salary whose HR master 'Basic Salary' is 0), not the HR master 'Basic Salary'. Compare as numbers (2 decimals). For an apprentice or intern, Sueldo is the contractual monthly maintenance support fixed in the apprenticeship contract - remuneration of an employee under Law 2466 of 2025 art. 21 (see NIE157) - taken from the same pay-run field 'Salary Assigned'; the amount actually paid in the month is reported in ApoyoSost (NIE157), not in SueldoTrabajado (NIE070).

Where the HR master holds several start dates ('Hire Date', 'Original Hire Date', 'Adjusted Service Date'), 'Hire Date' is the one used: the annex defines this attribute as the date the worker joined the employer for the current relationship, and TiempoLaborado (NIE006) is computed from the same date. A rehired employee starts a new relationship, whose earlier contract was closed with its own FechaRetiro (NIE003), so 'Original Hire Date' and 'Adjusted Service Date' are not used; a transfer between positions within the same employer does not end the relationship and does not change this date.

Nomina Electronica Spec!L82	Payroll.Service_Days [Not an amount field -- a day/hour count; no WTC code applies by design]
Nomina Electronica Spec!M82	COMPOSITE VALUE - construction rule. Cumulative service with the employer expressed in DAYS on the Colombian commercial-year convention fixed by Technical Annex s.8.3.1: 1 year = 360 days, 1 month = 30 days. The annex's own worked example is 5 years + 3 months + 18 days = (5 x 360) + (3 x 30) + 18 = 1908.00. Sub-fields: [years x 360] + [months x 30] + [odd days]. PERSISTENCE: recomputed every month from FechaIngreso (NIE002) to the settlement end; it is cumulative service, NOT days worked in the period (that is DiasTrabajados, NIE069). |  | Source: computed from the date used for NIE002 ('Hire Date'); never mix two dates between NIE002 and this field. Always the 360/30 construction above, never a calendar day-count, and always with two decimals.

Nomina Electronica Spec!P82	Two decimals, per the annex example (1908.00).

Nomina Electronica Spec!K85	30
Nomina Electronica Spec!L85	Payroll.Days_Worked [Not an amount field -- a day/hour count; no WTC code applies by design]
Nomina Electronica Spec!M85	Days actually worked and paid in the period, on the 30-day commercial month. Distinct from TiempoLaborado (NIE006), which is cumulative service. Reduced by unpaid leave, by suspension and by a mid-month hire or leave. |  | Source: pay-run field 'Days Worked'. Unpaid leave and suspension reduce it; a mid-month hire or leaver reduces it to the days of the relationship in the period. Do not use WT 63812 (gross month count) or WT 63953 (PILA day count), and do not derive it from the leave register, which counts business days and includes compensated vacation and episodes of earlier months. Paid absence days (vacation, incapacity, maternity / paternity and paid leave) are NOT subtracted: they are paid days, reported in their own Devengados blocks (Population Rules, DiasTrabajados). Only unpaid leave, suspension and lawful strike reduce the count.

Nomina Electronica Spec!L86	Payroll.Basic_Salary_Paid [WTC: CO-SALARIO-BASICO]
Nomina Electronica Spec!M86	The basic salary actually earned for the days worked. Moves with DiasTrabajados; contrast Sueldo (NIE062), which is the static contractual figure. This is the single largest input to the ET art. 388 depurated withholding base and to the PILA contribution base. |  | Source: wage types 62100 'Ordinary Salary- 0003' and 62140 'Integral Salary- 0423'. Vacation days are not in this element (paid through WT 79401 -> NIE112). WT 62149 (intern support) does not belong here (-> NIE157). The salary arrears WTs 62101 'Arrears - Ordinary salary', 62141 'Arrears-Integral Salary' and 65820 are basic salary of an earlier period paid in this period: under the payment-date rule (Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.2.2) they are reported in the month of payment, in the element of the concept they settle, so an arrears of basic salary is reported in this element; the element attribute of each arrears wage type comes from the WTC.

Nomina Electronica Spec!L131	Payroll.Vacation_Days_Compensated [Not an amount field -- a day/hour count; no WTC code applies by design]
Nomina Electronica Spec!M131	Vacation days paid in cash instead of enjoyed (during employment, CST art. 189, or on termination, Ley 995 de 2005 art. 1). A SEPARATE element from vacation taken: vacation paid in cash is NOT part of the health, pension and occupational-risk IBC (Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, exp. 26941 of 2024; unification ruling exp. 25185 of 2021, rule 1), whereas for enjoyed vacation days health and pension are contributed on the IBC of the month before the vacation starts (DUR 780 de 2016 art. 3.2.5.1; Consejo de Estado, Sección Cuarta, exp. 25958 of 2022). Both ARE part of the parafiscal base (CCF and SENA - Ley 21 de 1982 art. 17; ICBF - Ley 89 de 1988 art. 1 par. 1; Consejo de Estado, Sala de Consulta, concept 2027 of 2010). The engine must not merge them.

The annual consignment to the severance funds appears in payroll as the informative WT 65805 / 25827 'Payment Severance Funds Inf.'. That consignment is reported in this element in the document of the month in which it is consigned (by 14 February, Law 50 of 1990 art. 99 num. 3): it is a credit in account of the employee's cesantias (ET art. 27 num. 3 - cesantias are realised when paid to the employee or consigned to the fund), and this element feeds the three Form 220 buckets - paid, consigned, traditional regime (Population Rules, Cesantias). As an informative earning it is reported together with its offsetting deduction WT 25827 (NIE195), so ComprobanteTotal still equals the cash net paid.

Nomina Electronica Spec!H212	/NominaIndividual/DevengadosTotal
Nomina Electronica Spec!I212	Var
Nomina Electronica Spec!J212	N (decimal 2)
Nomina Electronica Spec!K212	12000000.00
Nomina Electronica Spec!L212	SUM(all Devengados elements)
Nomina Electronica Spec!M212	Sum of every element in the Devengados block. Item 6 of the mandatory content of Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.3.1. Input [4] to the CUNE, where it must be TRUNCATED to two decimals - truncated, not rounded, a distinction that catches many implementations. Bidirectional set with 'Rounding residual' (NIE186), which is present only where round-half-to-even rounding of the individual concepts leaves this total differing from the sum of its parts. |  | Source: the total is the sum of the elements reported. Payroll groups wage types as 'Entitlements', 'Deductions' and 'Non Cash Earnings/Deductions': include all 'Entitlements' wage types mapped to Devengados elements, plus only those informative 'Non Cash' earnings explicitly mapped (NIE193 / NIE154); employer contributions and bases in the Non Cash grouping are never included. A wage type that is in the gross but in no element, or in an element but not in the total, is a mapping error. A negative earning line (for example the reversal WTs 62216, 62540) is netted against the positive lines of the same concept in the month, inside that concept's element, since no element accepts a negative value (Population Rules, amount format); the concept a reversal wage type belongs to comes from its WTC attribute.

File Structure!A1	FILE STRUCTURE AND TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS
File Structure!A2	═══ FILE FORMAT ═══
File Structure!A3	File type
File Structure!B3	XML 1.0. The signature area uses OASIS UBL 2.1; every other section uses DIAN's own structure as published in the Technical Annex. This is a REAL XML submission with a published schema - not a portal form - so the wire format below is the actual submission format, not a logical model.
File Structure!A4	Schema
File Structure!B4	XSD version 1.0.6, referenced by Technical Annex version 1.0. Root elements: NominaIndividual (TipoXML 102) and NominaIndividualDeAjuste (TipoXML 103).
File Structure!A5	Character encoding
File Structure!B5	UTF-8 ONLY. Annex s.9.7.1. Any other encoding is rejected at unzip time.
File Structure!A6	Transport packaging
File Structure!B6	The XML is placed in a ZIP; the ZIP is base64-encoded; the base64 string is the single wcf:contentFile parameter of SendNominaSync.
File Structure!A7	Maximum size
File Structure!B7	500 KB for the transmitted payload.
File Structure!A8	Signature
File Structure!B8	XAdES-EPES enveloped signature inside /NominaIndividual/UBLExtensions/UBLExtension/ExtensionContent/ds:Signature, using an X.509 certificate from a certification authority accredited by ONAC. The ClaimedRole must be thirdparty or supplier (Annex s.7).
File Structure!A9	Decimal convention
File Structure!B9	Two decimals, decimal POINT, no thousand separators, no currency symbol. Rounding is round-half-to-even; validation tolerance is +/- 2.00.
File Structure!A10	Date / time convention
File Structure!B10	Dates AAAA-MM-DD. Times HH:MM:SS with the GMT offset, always -05:00 for Colombia (no daylight saving). Overtime start / end times use the 19-character form AAAA-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS.
File Structure!A11	═══ TRANSPORT - SOAP 1.2 WEB SERVICE (ANNEX S.9) ═══
File Structure!A12	Protocol
File Structure!B12	SOAP 1.2, Style/Encoding Document/Literal, over HTTPS with TLS 1.2 and MUTUAL authentication by digital certificate.
File Structure!A13	Security model
File Structure!B13	WS-Security 1.0 (OASIS) with X.509 Certificate Token Profile 1.1.
File Structure!A14	Namespaces
File Structure!B14	soap = http://www.w3.org/2003/05/soap-envelope ; wcf = http://wcf.dian.colombia
File Structure!A15	Submission operation
File Structure!B15	SendNominaSync - one parameter, wcf:contentFile, carrying the base64 of the ZIP. Synchronous.
File Structure!A16	Response action
File Structure!B16	http://wcf.dian.colombia/IWcfDianCustomerServices/SendNominaSyncResponse
File Structure!A17	Query operation
File Structure!B17	GetStatus - retrieves the status of a document already transmitted (Annex s.9.8).
File Structure!A18	Server-side checks, in order
File Structure!B18	1. ZIP is not empty. 2. ZIP is not corrupt. 3. The UBL 2.1 signed section exists. 4. The document matches the NominaIndividual or NominaIndividualDeAjuste XSD. 5. No error against the Annex's own structural rules. Only then is the ApplicationResponse produced.

File Structure!A133	Item 9
File Structure!B133	Payment or credit period (NIE004, NIE005).

File Structure!B134	Payment method and medium (NIE064, NIE065).
File Structure!A135	Item 11
File Structure!B135	Country, department and municipality of origin (NIE035, NIE036, NIE037).
File Structure!A136	Item 12
File Structure!B136	Content of the authorised technical annex (the whole document).
File Structure!A137	Item 13
File Structure!B137	CUNE - the unique electronic payroll code (NIE024).
File Structure!A138	═══ CONTROL MECHANISMS ═══
File Structure!A139	CUNE (Annex s.8.1)
File Structure!B139	SHA-384 of NumNE + FecNE + HorNE + ValDev + ValDed + ValTolNE + NitNE + DocEmp + TipoXML + Software-Pin + TipAmb, concatenated with no separators. Money inputs are TRUNCATED to two decimals, not rounded. Result is 96 lower-case hexadecimal characters.
File Structure!A140	SoftwareSC (Annex s.8.2)
File Structure!B140	SHA-384 of SoftwareID + PIN + document number. Changes with every document, unlike SoftwareID.
File Structure!A141	TiempoLaborado (Annex s.8.3.1)
File Structure!B141	Days on the 360/30 commercial convention: (years x 360) + (months x 30) + odd days. Annex example: 5 years + 3 months + 18 days = 1908.00.
File Structure!A142	QR (Annex s.13.1)
File Structure!B142	URL of the DIAN document-search service keyed on the CUNE. If a graphical representation is issued, the QR must be at least 2 cm x 2 cm on EVERY page.
File Structure!A143	Key validation rules
File Structure!B143	NIE022 - the Version literal must be exactly 'V1.0: Documento Soporte de Pago de Nómina Electrónica'. Global rule 90 - a document number may be transmitted only ONCE per employee. Global rule 92 - a document from a non-enabled issuer is rejected. Full rule set at Annex s.6.1.

Field Options!A65	(source conflict)
Field Options!B65	-
Field Options!C65	-
Field Options!D65	SOURCE CONFLICT. The annex percentages for codes 4, 5, 6 and 7 embed a 75% Sunday / public-holiday surcharge. Law 2101 of 2021 and Law 2466 of 2025 raise that surcharge to 80% from 1 July 2025 and to 90% from 1 JULY 2026, so from that date the statutory rate diverges from the printed annex value. The annex is still at version 1.0 and DIAN has not reissued table 5.5.5. This specification carries the annex values in @Porcentaje; the rate table is maintained as a configuration table, and the switch to the statutory value is made only on a written DIAN position.

Field Options!D98	Employee pension rate for NIE164. Employer 12%; total 16%. Set to 0.00 for an employed old-age pensioner (SubTipoTrabajador 01).
Field Options!A99	0.50

Field Options!A100	0.50 - 1.50

Field Options!C100	Fondo de solidaridad pensional - subcuenta de subsistencia

Field Options!A101	═══ SOLIDARITY PENSION FUND BANDS (LEY 100 ARTS. 20 AND 27 AS AMENDED BY LEY 797 DE 2003 ARTS. 7-8) ═══

Population Rules!A1	POPULATION RULES - HOW TO FILL EACH FIELD FROM PAYROLL
Population Rules!A2	Field Name
Population Rules!B2	Source System
Population Rules!C2	Population Logic
Population Rules!D2	Edge Cases
Population Rules!A3	═══ MANDATORY FIELD RULES ═══
Population Rules!A4	Version (NIE022)
Population Rules!B4	Configuration
Population Rules!C4	Emit the constant string 'V1.0: Documento Soporte de Pago de Nómina Electrónica' exactly, including accents and the colon-space. Never build it from a version variable, never trim, never transliterate. Validation rule NIE022 compares the whole literal.
Population Rules!D4	A configuration file saved as ASCII will silently strip the accent on 'Nomina' and every document will be rejected.
Population Rules!A5	Numero (NIE012)
Population Rules!B5	Engine sequence + configuration
Population Rules!C5	Concatenate the DIAN-authorised Prefix (NIE010) with the next unused Sequence number (NIE011), with no separator. Persist the last-used consecutive per prefix range in a transactional store, and increment it BEFORE transmission, never after, so that a failed transmission does not leave a reusable number. Global rule 90 permits a given number only once per employee.
Population Rules!D5	A rejected document consumes its number. On re-transmission the replacement document must carry a NEW consecutive. Never re-send a rejected number.
Population Rules!A6	CUNE (NIE024)
Population Rules!B6	Engine (computed)
Population Rules!C6	Compute SHA-384 over the eleven inputs concatenated with no separators, in the published order: Numero, FechaGen, HoraGen (with the -05:00 offset), DevengadosTotal, DeduccionesTotal, ComprobanteTotal, employer NIT (no DV), employee document number, TipoXML, the secret software PIN, and Ambiente. The three money inputs must be TRUNCATED to two decimals - not rounded - and written with a decimal point, no thousand separators and no currency symbol. Emit the result as 96 lower-case hexadecimal characters.
Population Rules!D6	The four classic failures: (1) rounding instead of truncating the money inputs; (2) omitting the -05:00 offset from HoraGen; (3) including the employer DV in NitNE; (4) using the SoftwareID instead of the PIN. All four produce a well-formed but wrong CUNE.
Population Rules!A7	SoftwareSC (NIE020)
Population Rules!B7	Engine (computed)
Population Rules!C7	Compute SHA-384 over SoftwareID + PIN + document number. Recompute for EVERY document, because the document number is an input. Do not cache it at software level.
Population Rules!D7	Caching SoftwareSC as if it were a constant is a common error - it is constant only for a single document.
Population Rules!A8	TiempoLaborado (NIE006)
Population Rules!B8	Engine (computed)
Population Rules!C8	Compute cumulative service from FechaIngreso to FechaLiquidacionFin on the Colombian commercial convention: 1 year = 360 days, 1 month = 30 days. Decompose the elapsed period into whole years, whole months and residual days, then evaluate (years x 360) + (months x 30) + days. Emit with two decimals. The annex's own example is 5 years + 3 months + 18 days = 1908.00.
Population Rules!D8	Do NOT use a calendar day-count. A naive (date2 - date1).days gives 1913 for the annex's own example instead of 1908, and DIAN will not flag it - the error surfaces only in a seniority dispute.
Population Rules!A9	DiasTrabajados (NIE069)
Population Rules!B9	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C9	Days actually worked and paid in the period on the 30-day commercial month. Start at 30 (or the number of days from a mid-month hire), then subtract unpaid-leave days (LicenciaNR), suspension days and lawful-strike days. Do NOT subtract incapacity, maternity or vacation days - those are paid and appear in their own devengado blocks.
Population Rules!D9	February always counts 30 days in Colombian payroll, rather than 28. A 31-day month also counts 30.
Population Rules!A10	═══ CRITICAL VALIDATION RULES ═══
Population Rules!A11	DevengadosTotal / DeduccionesTotal / ComprobanteTotal (NIE187-NIE189)
Population Rules!B11	Engine (computed)
Population Rules!C11	DevengadosTotal is the sum of every populated element in the Earnings block; DeduccionesTotal is the sum of every populated element in Deductions; ComprobanteTotal is DevengadosTotal minus DeduccionesTotal, adjusted for the Rounding residual. Each element is first rounded half-to-even to two decimals, then summed - not summed and then rounded. Where a residual remains, disclose it in Rounding (NIE186) rather than absorbing it into a concept. Validation tolerance is +/- 2.00.
Population Rules!D11	Summing unrounded values and rounding the total produces a figure that ties to the cent but does not match the sum of the printed lines on the payslip. Round first, then sum.
Population Rules!A12	Salary / non-salary paired elements
Population Rules!B12	Wage-type configuration
Population Rules!C12	Every paired element (ViaticoManuAlojS / NS, BonificacionS / NS, AuxilioS / NS, ConceptoS / NS, PagoS / NS, PagoAlimentacionS / NS, Primas Payment / PagoNS) is driven by the salary / non-salary attribute of the wage type in the Wage Type Catalogue (WTC). Never infer the split at reporting time from the amount, the description or the wage-type name.
Population Rules!D12	The 40% non-salary clamp of Ley 1393 de 2010 art. 30 depends on this split, as does the contribution base. An inference error propagates into PILA and into the employer cost.
Population Rules!A13	Employer contributions must never appear in Deductions
Population Rules!B13	Wage-type configuration
Population Rules!C13	The Deductions block reports only what was withheld FROM THE EMPLOYEE. The employer's 12% pension, 8.5% health, occupational-risk premium and the 9% payroll levies (SENA 2%, ICBF 3%, CCF 4%) are employer costs reported on PILA. Configure them on a cost, not a deduction, wage-type class so they cannot leak into this block.
Population Rules!D13	A single mis-classified employer wage type inflates DeduccionesTotal and understates ComprobanteTotal, which then disagrees with the actual bank payment.
Population Rules!A14	Solidarity fund must not be merged into pension
Population Rules!B14	Wage-type configuration
Population Rules!C14	FondoSP carries FOUR attributes - the solidarity rate and amount, and the subsistence rate and amount. They are validated independently of FondoPension. Configure the solidarity surcharge as its own wage type from the outset.
Population Rules!D14	This is the single most common Colombian mapping error. Merging produces a 5% 'pension' line that fails reconciliation against PILA fields 47, 51 and 52.
Population Rules!A15	Overtime cannot be aggregated
Population Rules!B15	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C15	Emit one element per contiguous block of hours of a single type. Split a block that crosses 21:00 or midnight, because the applicable code changes. Populate quantity, percentage and amount for every block, plus start and end times where the time register carries them.
Population Rules!D15	A block that crosses the 21:00 boundary but is emitted as a single HED element misstates both the code and the premium.
Population Rules!A16	═══ AMOUNT FORMAT RULES ═══
Population Rules!A17	All monetary elements
Population Rules!B17	Engine (formatting)
Population Rules!C17	Two decimals, decimal POINT, no thousand separator, no currency symbol, no leading plus sign. Rounding is round-half-to-even (NTC 3711 / JIS Z 8401), not half-up. Negative values are not used in the accrued or deducted blocks; only Rounding (NIE186) may legitimately be negative.
Population Rules!D17	A locale-aware formatter will emit a comma decimal separator in an es-CO locale and the document will fail schema validation.
Population Rules!A18	Three rounding conventions coexist
Population Rules!B18	Engine (formatting)
Population Rules!C18	The XML uses two decimals round-half-to-even. PILA rounds the base UP to the peso and each contribution UP to the next multiple of 100. Form 350 rounds to the nearest 1,000. The same health contribution therefore legitimately appears as three different numbers in three artefacts. Store the unrounded value once and apply each convention at output time.
Population Rules!D18	Never display the PILA figure on the payslip as if it were the deduction. Where the PILA figure is shown on the payslip, it must be a separate, labelled line (CCG Module 10.3).
Population Rules!A19	Percentage attributes
Population Rules!B19	Configuration
Population Rules!C19	Rate attributes (NIE077 and the other six @Porcentaje, NIE161, NIE164, NIE167, NIE169, NIE121, NIE171) carry the rate itself, expressed to two decimals - 4.00 rather than 0.04, 25.00 rather than 0.25.
Population Rules!D19	Emitting 0.04 for the 4% health rate passes schema validation and produces a document that is internally inconsistent with @Deduccion.
Population Rules!A20	═══ PAYROLL-SPECIFIC POPULATION RULES ═══
Population Rules!A21	RetencionFuente (NIE177) - RESIDENT
Population Rules!B21	Payroll engine (tax)
Population Rules!C21	Build the ET art. 388 depurated base: gross taxable payments, less the mandatory health, pension and solidarity-fund contributions (ingresos no constitutivos de renta), less the art. 387 deductions elected by the employee (dependants at 10% of gross capped at 32 UVT per month, prepaid health up to 16 UVT per month, housing-loan interest up to 100 UVT per month), less the 25% exempt income of art. 206 num. 10. Apply the two caps: deductions plus exempt income may not exceed 40% of gross less non-constitutive income, nor 1,340 UVT per year (the annual limit is apportioned at 111.6667 UVT per month, a configurable apportionment rule). Convert the resulting base to UVT at 52,374, read the art. 383 table, convert back to pesos, and approximate to the nearest 1,000 under ET art. 868. Under Procedure 2 apply instead the fixed percentage recomputed each June and December on the preceding twelve months divided by 13.
Population Rules!D21	Exclude severance savings and the interest on them from the monthly base under BOTH procedures (art. 206 num. 4). Under Procedure 1 the statutory minimum service bonus gets its OWN separate read of the table (art. 385) and does not merge into the ordinary base. For a sub-30-day period apply the art. 385 rule: gross the payments to 30 days, read the table for a PERCENTAGE, then apply that percentage to the actual smaller payment - never read the table for an amount on the grossed-up figure.
Population Rules!A22	RetencionFuente (NIE177) - NON-RESIDENT
Population Rules!B22	Payroll engine (tax)
Population Rules!C22	Apply a FLAT 20% to the GROSS payment under ET art. 408. Do not depurate: no deduction of the mandatory contributions, no art. 387 deductions, no 25% exempt income, no tax-free band, no Procedure 1 or 2 election. This applies to salary, to the service bonus, to severance savings, to dismissal indemnity, to equity income and to benefits in kind alike. Carry a residence flag with an effective date on the employee record, re-evaluate it EVERY period against the art. 10 test (more than 183 days in any 365-day period), and switch to the art. 383 table PROSPECTIVELY from the payment following the date the test is met.
Population Rules!D22	Never apply the switch retroactively and never true up the earlier months through payroll - DIAN Concept 011108 of 29 June 2026 expressly rejects anticipating future residence. The employee reconciles on their own return. Where the work is performed ENTIRELY outside Colombia the income is foreign-source and there is no withholding at all; LugarTrabajoPais (NIE050) is the field that signals this. Treaty relief is applied ONLY on written client instruction with a residence certificate.
Population Rules!A23	AuxilioTransporte (NIE071)
Population Rules!B23	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C23	Emit only where the employee's salary does not exceed 2 SMMLV (COP 3,501,810 in 2026). The 2026 amount is COP 249,095 per month, prorated by days worked. Where the employee is above the threshold, OMIT the element rather than emitting zero.
Population Rules!D23	The allowance is NOT salary for contribution purposes but IS included in the base of cesantias, prima and the severance indemnity (Ley 1 de 1963 art. 7). Carrying a single 'transport' wage type with one base flag will get one of the two treatments wrong.
Population Rules!A24	Cesantias (NIE120) and PagoIntereses (NIE122)
Population Rules!B24	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C24	Cesantias accrue at one month's salary per year of service, on salary plus the transport allowance. Interest accrues at 12% per annum on the 31 December balance and is paid TO THE EMPLOYEE during January. Where the employer has adopted the Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 64 option, the 8.33% is deposited monthly and the 1% interest paid monthly, by written agreement - a configurable engine option.
Population Rules!D24	Severance savings are EXCLUDED from the monthly withholding base but must still be reported here. They split THREE ways downstream - paid to the employee, deposited to the fund, and under the pre-1991 traditional regime - for Form 220 casillas 47-49 and Format 2276 numerals 18-20. Maintain the three buckets from the outset.
Population Rules!A25	Meal payments (NIE153 / NIE154)
Population Rules!B25	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C25	Maintain the ET art. 387-1 split independently of the S / NS split: the amount UP TO 41 UVT per month (COP 2,147,334 in 2026) where salary does not exceed 310 UVT (COP 16,235,940) goes to Format 2276 numeral 32, and the EXCESS goes to numeral 8. Both are still reported in the XML as PagoAlimentacionS or NS.
Population Rules!D25	Two orthogonal splits apply to the same amount - salary / non-salary for the XML and contributions, and under-41-UVT / excess for the information return. Model them as two independent attributes, not one.
Population Rules!A26	Incapacity (NIE123-NIE127)
Population Rules!B26	Payroll engine
Population Rules!C26	Emit one Incapacity element per episode with its dates, day count, type code and amount. For type 1 (comun) the employer bears days 1-2 and the EPS funds from day 3; for types 2 and 3 the ARL funds from day one at 100%. The employer ADVANCES the benefit and recovers it from the institution, so the amount here is what the employee received.
Population Rules!D26	The first-two-days rate is a configuration constant, default 66.67% (CST art. 227: two-thirds of salary for the first 90 days); the primary source does not state the rate. The one-minimum-wage floor on incapacity pay follows settled practice and is a configuration constant of the incapacity calculation.
Population Rules!A27	AltoRiesgoPension (NIE043)
Population Rules!B27	Employee master data
Population Rules!C27	Set true where the employee performs an activity listed in Decree-Law 2090 of 2003. The flag is always emitted (1-1); write the literal true or false, rather than 1 or 0.
Population Rules!D27	The ADDITIONAL employer pension contribution for high-risk work - ten percentage points above the standard rate, borne by the employer (Decree-Law 2090 of 2003 art. 5; the Resolution 2388 rate table shows 26% total against the standard 16%) - and its base are held in the contribution-rate configuration table, maintained from the primary source in force for the year.
Population Rules!A28	═══ CROSS-FIELD VALIDATIONS ═══
Population Rules!A29	NIE014 / NIE015 and NIE051 / NIE052
Population Rules!B29	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C29	The five-digit municipality code must begin with the two-digit department code. Validate this for both the generation location and the place of work before transmission.
Population Rules!D29	DIVIPOLA codes are re-used across departments in their last three digits; only the full five-digit code is unique.
Population Rules!A30	NIE065 and NIE066 / NIE067 / NIE068
Population Rules!B30	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C30	Where the payment method is a bank-mediated medium (42, 45, 46, 47, 48, 49), the bank, account type and account number must all be present. Where it is 10 (Efectivo), all three must be absent.
Population Rules!D30	Colombia does NOT use IBAN. Account numbers are numeric, of bank-specific length, with no national check digit, so no structural validation is possible - only a bank-specific rule or pre-notification.
Population Rules!A31	NIE199 and NIE204
Population Rules!B31	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C31	Where the Change event element is present, CUNENov is mandatory inside it; where Change event is absent, CUNENov must not appear. Bidirectional.
Population Rules!D31	A Change event without a referenced CUNE breaks the cross-reference chain for the employment relationship.
Population Rules!A32	NIAE214 and the Replace / Delete blocks
Population Rules!B32	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C32	TipoNota = 1 requires the Replace block and forbids Delete; TipoNota = 2 requires Delete and forbids Replace. Mutually exclusive.
Population Rules!D32	The Delete CUNE uses ValDev = ValDed = ValTol = 0.00 and DocEmp = 0 (Annex s.8.1.1.4). A shared CUNE routine that reads the real totals will produce a wrong hash for cancellations.
Population Rules!A33	NIE041 and NIE042 and NIE164
Population Rules!B33	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C33	Where SubTipoTrabajador (NIE042) = 01 (employed old-age pensioner), the pension rate NIE164 must be 0.00 and no pension deduction may be emitted.
Population Rules!D33	The PILA equivalent (subtipo 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9 or 12 forces field 46 to zero) is broader; map, do not copy.
Population Rules!A34	NIE157 and NIE061 and NIE041
Population Rules!B34	Engine (validation)
Population Rules!C34	Where the apprentice maintenance support (NIE157) is populated, the contract type must be 4 (Aprendizaje) and the worker type must be 12 or 19.
Population Rules!D34	Ley 2466 de 2025 art. 21 made the apprenticeship a labour contract from 2026, so an apprentice now also generates a full PILA settlement - not only the support payment.
Population Rules!A35	Cross-report reconciliation
Population Rules!B35	Engine (reconciliation)
Population Rules!C35	Monthly: SUM(NIE177) over all employees must equal Form 350 casillas 93 plus 94 after the nearest-1,000 rounding. SUM(NIE163), SUM(NIE166) and SUM(NIE168 + NIE170) must equal the PILA totals for health, pension and the solidarity fund, allowing for PILA's round-up-to-100. Annually: the accumulation of each Earnings element must equal the corresponding Form 220 casilla and Format 2276 numeral.
Population Rules!D35	Run the reconciliation BEFORE transmission. A mismatch discovered after validation can only be put right by an adjustment note, which consumes a new document number.

Example Analysis!A24	═══ PERIOD ═══
Example Analysis!A25	2021-01-11
Example Analysis!B25	FechaIngreso (NIE002)
Example Analysis!C25	Hired 11 January 2021.
Example Analysis!D25	Drives TiempoLaborado and the cesantias accrual.
Example Analysis!A26	2026-03-01 / 2026-03-31
Example Analysis!B26	FechaLiquidacionInicio / Fin (NIE004 / NIE005)
Example Analysis!C26	The document settles the whole of March 2026.
Example Analysis!D26	One document per employee per month, whatever the pay cycle (Res. DIAN 000227 de 2025 art. 1.5.3.2.2).
Example Analysis!A27	1850.00
Example Analysis!B27	TiempoLaborado (NIE006)
Example Analysis!C27	Cumulative service on the 360/30 convention: 5 years + 1 month + 20 days from 11 January 2021 to 31 March 2026 = (5 x 360) + (1 x 30) + 20 = 1850.00.
Example Analysis!D27	A calendar day-count would give 1905 and be wrong. The annex's own example is 5y 3m 18d = 1908.00.
Example Analysis!A28	30
Example Analysis!B28	DiasTrabajados (NIE069)
Example Analysis!C28	A full commercial month.
Example Analysis!D28	February would also be 30, and so would a 31-day month. Reduced only by unpaid leave, suspension or strike.
Example Analysis!A29	2026-03-31
Example Analysis!B29	FechaPago (NIE203)
Example Analysis!C29	Single payment on 31 March 2026.
Example Analysis!D29	Cardinality 1-N. A fortnightly payroll would carry two occurrences here.
Example Analysis!A30	═══ ACCRUED - DEVENGADOS ═══
Example Analysis!A31	12000000.00
Example Analysis!B31	SueldoTrabajado (NIE070)
Example Analysis!C31	Full month's basic salary.
Example Analysis!D31	The only devengado in this simple month. It is the entire ET art. 388 gross and the entire PILA contribution base.
Example Analysis!A32	(absent)
Example Analysis!B32	AuxilioTransporte (NIE071)
Example Analysis!C32	Not payable: COP 12,000,000 exceeds 2 SMMLV (COP 3,501,810).
Example Analysis!D32	OMITTED, not zero-filled. Had it been payable it would be COP 249,095 - not salary for contributions, but in the base of cesantias, prima and the severance indemnity.
Example Analysis!A33	12000000.00
Example Analysis!B33	DevengadosTotal (NIE187)
Example Analysis!C33	Sum of the Devengados block.
Example Analysis!D33	Art. 5 item 6. CUNE input [4], truncated to two decimals.
Example Analysis!A34	═══ DEDUCTED - DEDUCCIONES ═══
Example Analysis!A35	4.00 / 480000.00
Example Analysis!B35	Salud @Porcentaje / @Deduccion (NIE161 / NIE163)
Example Analysis!C35	Employee health at 4% of COP 12,000,000.
Example Analysis!D35	The employer's 8.5% does NOT appear here - and in this case is nil anyway, because the employee earns under 10 SMMLV and the employer is exonerated under ET art. 114-1. The PILA figure for the same contribution is COP 480,000 rounded UP to the next 100, which happens to coincide here.
Example Analysis!A36	4.00 / 480000.00
Example Analysis!B36	FondoPension @Porcentaje / @Deduccion (NIE164 / NIE166)
Example Analysis!C36	Employee pension at 4% of COP 12,000,000.
Example Analysis!D36	The employer's 12% is a PILA cost, not a deduction. Total pension into the system is 16% = COP 1,920,000.
Example Analysis!A37	0.50 / 60000.00
Example Analysis!B37	FondoSP @Porcentaje / @DeduccionSP (NIE167 / NIE168)
Example Analysis!C37	Solidarity sub-account at 0.5% of COP 12,000,000 (50% of the additional 1%), because the base is at or above 4 SMMLV.
Example Analysis!D37	SEPARATE from pension. Merging this into FondoPension to show a 5% pension line is the single most common Colombian mapping error.
Example Analysis!A38	0.50 / 60000.00
Example Analysis!B38	FondoSP @PorcentajeSub / @DeduccionSub (NIE169 / NIE170)
Example Analysis!C38	Subsistence sub-account at 0.5% of COP 12,000,000 (the other 50% of the additional 1%); no band surcharge because the base is below 16 SMMLV.
Example Analysis!D38	Both sub-accounts start at 4 SMMLV; only the subsistence one rises from 16 SMMLV (Ley 100 art. 27 as amended by Ley 797 art. 8).
Example Analysis!A39	617000.00
Example Analysis!B39	RetencionFuente (NIE177)
Example Analysis!C39	Withholding computed under Procedure 1: gross 12,000,000 less non-constitutive contributions 1,080,000 = 10,920,000; less 25% exempt income 2,730,000 (within both the 40% and the apportioned 1,340-UVT caps) = base 8,190,000; = 156.375 UVT; band 150-360 UVT at 28%: (156.375 - 150) x 0.28 + 10 = 11.785 UVT; x 52,374 = COP 617,232, approximated to the nearest 1,000 under ET art. 868 = COP 617,000.
Example Analysis!D39	NON-RESIDENT CONTRAST: the same employee, before acquiring residence, would be withheld at the flat 20% of ET art. 408 on the GROSS - 20% x 12,000,000 = COP 2,400,000, with no depuration and no exempt income. Nearly four times the resident figure.
Example Analysis!A40	1697000.00
Example Analysis!B40	DeduccionesTotal (NIE188)
Example Analysis!C40	480,000 + 480,000 + 120,000 + 617,000.
Example Analysis!D40	Art. 5 item 7. CUNE input [5].
Example Analysis!A41	═══ TOTALS AND CONTROL ═══
Example Analysis!A42	(absent)
Example Analysis!B42	Rounding (NIE186)
Example Analysis!C42	No residual: every concept is a whole peso, so the round-half-to-even sums tie exactly.
Example Analysis!D42	Where a residual arises it must be disclosed here, within the +/- 2.00 tolerance, not absorbed into a concept.
Example Analysis!A43	10303000.00
Example Analysis!B43	ComprobanteTotal (NIE189)
Example Analysis!C43	12,000,000 - 1,697,000.
Example Analysis!D43	Art. 5 item 8. CUNE input [6]. This is the amount that must match the bank payment file.
Example Analysis!A44	16560dc8...f3bac2
Example Analysis!B44	CUNE (NIE024)
Example Analysis!C44	SHA-384 over NE1042 + 2026-04-05 + 10:53:10-05:00 + 12000000.00 + 1697000.00 + 10303000.00 + 900123456 + 1020304050 + 102 + <PIN> + 1.
Example Analysis!D44	96 hexadecimal characters. The PIN never appears in the XML. Any change to any of the eleven inputs yields a different document.

Example Analysis!D47	The depurated base, not the gross.

Sample File!A173	    <RetencionFuente>2400000.00</RetencionFuente>   <!-- ET art. 408: 20% x GROSS -->
Sample File!A174	  </Deducciones>

Sample File!A177	  <DeduccionesTotal>3480000.00</DeduccionesTotal>

Sample File!A180	Flat 20% of ET art. 408 applied to the GROSS COP 12,000,000, with no art. 388
Sample File!A181	depuration, no 25% exempt income, no art. 387 deductions and no tax-free band
Sample File!A182	(DIAN Concepto 011108 of 29 June 2026). Where the work were performed ENTIRELY
Sample File!A183	outside Colombia, LugarTrabajoPais would not be CO and there would be NO
Sample File!A184	withholding at all (DIAN Official letter 28380 of 2019).

Sample File!A187	5. ADJUSTMENT NOTE SKELETON (NominaIndividualDeAjuste, TipoXML 103)
Sample File!A188	-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sample File!A189	<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
Sample File!A190	<NominaIndividualDeAjuste>
Sample File!A191	  <TipoNota>1</TipoNota>                    <!-- 1 Reemplazar | 2 Eliminar -->
Sample File!A192	  <Reemplazar>
Sample File!A193	    <!-- the ENTIRE NominaIndividual structure repeated with the correct values,
Sample File!A194	         under NIAE-prefixed codes, carrying its OWN CUNE computed on the same
Sample File!A195	         eleven inputs taken from this Reemplazar block -->
Sample File!A196	  </Reemplazar>
Sample File!A197	</NominaIndividualDeAjuste>
Sample File!A199	For TipoNota = 2 the Delete block is used instead, and its CUNE is computed
Sample File!A200	with ValDev = ValDed = ValTol = 0.00 and DocEmp = 0 (Annex s.8.1.1.4). A shared
Sample File!A201	CUNE routine that reads the real totals will produce a wrong hash for a
Sample File!A202	cancellation - this is a deliberate divergence in the specification, not an
Sample File!A203	error in it.
